<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d9/Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg/960px-Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg.png" decoding="async" width="690" height="350" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d9/Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg/1280px-Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/d/d9/Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg/1920px-Blank_Map_World_Secondary_Political_Divisions.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="512" data-file-height="260" /></a><figcaption>World first-and-second degree administrative levels</figcaption></figure>
<figure class="mw-default-size mw-halign-left" typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/46/Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg/960px-Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg.png" decoding="async" width="690" height="354" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/46/Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg/1280px-Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg.png 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/4/46/Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg/1920px-Map_of_unitary_and_federal_states.svg.png 2x" data-file-width="863" data-file-height="443" /></a><figcaption>A world map distinguishing countries of the world as federations (<span style="color:#00e000"><b>green</b></span>) from <a href="/wiki/Unitary_state" title="Unitary state">unitary states</a> (<span style="color: #0000b0"><b>blue</b></span>) <hr /> <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959" /><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#0000b0; color:white;-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Unitary_state" title="Unitary state">Unitary states</a></div> <link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r981673959" /><div class="legend"><span class="legend-color mw-no-invert" style="background-color:#00e000; color:black;-webkit-print-color-adjust: exact; print-color-adjust: exact;">&#160;</span>&#160;<a href="/wiki/Federations" class="mw-redirect" title="Federations">Federations</a></div></figcaption></figure>
<div style="clear:both;" class=""></div>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Public_administration">Public administration</h2></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1320445320" /><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Public_administration" title="Public administration">Public administration</a></div>
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:People_at_Tax_Office_1945_(8335C;_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1).tif" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b2/People_at_Tax_Office_1945_%288335C%3B_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1%29.tif/lossy-page1-330px-People_at_Tax_Office_1945_%288335C%3B_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1%29.tif.jpg" decoding="async" width="300" height="211" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b2/People_at_Tax_Office_1945_%288335C%3B_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1%29.tif/lossy-page1-500px-People_at_Tax_Office_1945_%288335C%3B_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1%29.tif.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/b/b2/People_at_Tax_Office_1945_%288335C%3B_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1%29.tif/lossy-page1-960px-People_at_Tax_Office_1945_%288335C%3B_JOKAHBL3C_F10-1%29.tif.jpg 2x" data-file-width="6511" data-file-height="4585" /></a><figcaption>Tax office in <a href="/wiki/Finland" title="Finland">Finland</a>, 1945</figcaption></figure>	
<figure typeof="mw:File/Thumb"><a href="/wiki/File:Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg" class="mw-file-description"><img src="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/60/Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg/330px-Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg" decoding="async" width="300" height="225" class="mw-file-element" srcset="//upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/60/Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg/500px-Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg 1.5x, //upload.wikimedia.org/wikipedia/commons/thumb/6/60/Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg/960px-Primeiro_Of%C3%ADcio_de_Registro_Civil_de_Santo_Andr%C3%A9.jpg 2x" data-file-width="4032" data-file-height="3024" /></a><figcaption>1st <a href="/wiki/Vital_records" class="mw-redirect" title="Vital records">Vital Records</a> Office of <a href="/wiki/Santo_Andr%C3%A9,_S%C3%A3o_Paulo" title="Santo André, São Paulo">Santo André, São Paulo</a>, <a href="/wiki/Brazil" title="Brazil">Brazil</a>, 2019</figcaption></figure>
<p><a href="/wiki/Governmental_property" class="mw-redirect" title="Governmental property">Governmental property</a>, <a href="/wiki/State-owned_enterprise" title="State-owned enterprise">state-owned enterprises</a>, <a href="/wiki/Public_services" class="mw-redirect" title="Public services">public services</a>, <a href="/wiki/Civil_servants" class="mw-redirect" title="Civil servants">civil servants</a> and employees of the government together compose the <a href="/wiki/Public_sector" title="Public sector">public sector</a> of the economy. In modern <a href="/wiki/Developed_country" title="Developed country">developed countries</a>, public services (or services of general interest) often include:<sup id="cite&#95;ref-53" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-53"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>50<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
<style data-mw-deduplicate="TemplateStyles:r1184024115">.mw-parser-output .div-col{margin-top:0.3em;column-width:30em}.mw-parser-output .div-col-small{font-size:90%}.mw-parser-output .div-col-rules{column-rule:1px solid #aaa}.mw-parser-output .div-col dl,.mw-parser-output .div-col ol,.mw-parser-output .div-col ul{margin-top:0}.mw-parser-output .div-col li,.mw-parser-output .div-col dd{page-break-inside:avoid;break-inside:avoid-column}</style><div class="div-col" style="column-width: 30em;">
<ul><li><a href="/wiki/Court" title="Court">Courts</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Education" title="Education">Education</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Electricity" title="Electricity">Electricity</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Emergency_service" title="Emergency service">Emergency services</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Environmental_protection" title="Environmental protection">Environmental protection</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Health_care" title="Health care">Health care</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Mail" title="Mail">Mail</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Military" title="Military">Military</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Policing" class="mw-redirect" title="Policing">Policing</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Building" title="Building">Public buildings</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Public_broadcasting" title="Public broadcasting">Public broadcasting</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Public_library" title="Public library">Public libraries</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Urban_park" title="Urban park">Public parks</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Public_utility" title="Public utility">Public utilities</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Public_transport" title="Public transport">Public transportation</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Social_services" title="Social services">Social services</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/State_school" title="State school">State school</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Telecommunications" title="Telecommunications">Telecommunications</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Transportation_infrastructure" class="mw-redirect" title="Transportation infrastructure">Transportation infrastructure</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Urban_planning" title="Urban planning">Urban planning</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Waste_management" title="Waste management">Waste management</a></li>
<li><a href="/wiki/Water_supply_network" title="Water supply network">Water supply network</a></li></ul>
</div>
<p>In <a href="/wiki/Developing_country" title="Developing country">developing countries</a>, public services tend to be much less well developed.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (July 2025)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup> For example, water services might only be available to the <a href="/wiki/Wealth" title="Wealth">wealthy</a> <a href="/wiki/Middle_class" title="Middle class">middle class</a>. For political reasons, the service is often <a href="/wiki/Subsidy" title="Subsidy">subsidized</a>, which reduces the finance potentially available for expansion to poorer communities.<sup class="noprint Inline-Template Template-Fact" style="white-space:nowrap;">&#91;<i><a href="/wiki/Wikipedia:Citation_needed" title="Wikipedia:Citation needed"><span title="This claim needs references to reliable sources. (July 2017)">citation needed</span></a></i>&#93;</sup> The United Nations <a href="/wiki/Sustainable_Development_Goal_5" title="Sustainable Development Goal 5">Sustainable Development Goal 5</a> is a global initiative that aims to influence the provision of public services and infrastructure to marginalized demographics.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-54" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-54"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>51<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p><p>The mid-twentieth century saw the rise of German <a href="/wiki/Sociology" title="Sociology">sociologist</a> <a href="/wiki/Max_Weber" title="Max Weber">Max Weber</a>'s theory of <a href="/wiki/Bureaucracy" title="Bureaucracy">bureaucracy</a>, bringing about a substantive interest in the theoretical aspects of public administration.
</p>
<div class="mw-heading mw-heading2"><h2 id="Public_policy">Public policy</h2></div>
<link rel="mw-deduplicated-inline-style" href="mw-data:TemplateStyles:r1320445320" /><div role="note" class="hatnote navigation-not-searchable">Main article: <a href="/wiki/Public_policy" title="Public policy">Public policy</a></div>
<p>Public policy can be considered the sum of a government's direct and indirect activities.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-55" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-55"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>52<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> It includes various aspects of life such as education, health care, employment, finance, economics, transportation, and all over elements of society.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-56" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-56"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>53<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup> Public policy making can be characterized as a dynamic, complex, and interactive system through which public problems are identified and resolved through the creation of new policy or reform of existing policy.<sup id="cite&#95;ref-Analysing&#95;Public&#95;Policy&#95;57-0" class="reference"><a href="#cite_note-Analysing_Public_Policy-57"><span class="cite-bracket">&#91;</span>54<span class="cite-bracket">&#93;</span></a></sup>
</p>
